## assistant

The research is done. Both of us researched separately with Firecrawl, graded each other's lists, and argued to one ranked list. Everything is filed in `research/2026-09-23-classroom-home-dashboard-research/`, and the brainstorm file is updated.

**The agreed 7.** All of them add to v3; none replace it.
1. **Diagnosis to action.** The main insight doesn't just say "Necessary Assumption is weak". It recommends the right kind of work:
   - a bootcamp lesson if the section analysis says fundamentals
   - a guided drill if it says process
   - Read, Recall, Colour if it says reading
   - timed practice if it says test-taking

   The app already produces these four categories for every section.
2. **Coaching attached to the work.** Your signed note on a pinned insight, an "Ask" button that sends you the evidence behind it, and a short pre-session brief the student prepares for you.
3. **A "Today" sequence.** One optional, resumable path through the day's work, with a clear finish line.
4. **Review, then a fresh check.** Redo the missed question, then try an unseen question of the same kind. That separates "remembered the answer" from "learned the skill".
5. **Sure but wrong.** Questions they were confident about and missed, using the confidence ratings the app already saves.
6. **Honest visuals.** Keep the bubbles, but size them correctly, show the counts, and use plainer wording: "Most misses in your recent LR practice" instead of "costing you points".
7. **Fast, stable and accessible.** Instant loading, clear stale and error states, a real phone layout, screen readers, and your accommodation timers respected. This is a bar every feature must meet, not a separate feature.

**Where Codex beat me:**
- I withdrew my blind-review verdict card. A score gap alone doesn't prove whether the problem is speed or understanding, so the gap becomes evidence inside #1 instead.
- Codex also caught that "Smart Drill on one question type" isn't possible. It has to be a filtered drill.

**Where Codex missed something I caught:**
- Your view of exactly what a student sees.
- A "last updated" on every number.
- Codex took my signed-note idea.

**Round 10**

**Q1 - What comes first**: Codex and I disagree. It says diagnosis to action, because students need a clear next step between sessions. I say coaching attached to the work, because the 1:1 relationship is what students pay for. Which leads the page?

Recommended: coaching. Your note sits on top of the diagnosis, so both show, but your voice leads.

---

**Q2 - The role of "Today"**: Should it be the main path that holds the other features (my view), or an optional shortcut while each feature keeps its own spot (Codex's view)?

Recommended: optional shortcut for the first build. Codex is right that it has to prove itself, and the page stays useful without it.

---

**Q3 - Your part in diagnosis to action**: it needs a short list you approve, matching each of the four problem types to the lesson or drill you'd assign. Will you write it, starting with about 4 to 8 entries?

Recommended: yes. I'd draft it from your bootcamps and guided drills, and you'd correct it.

---

**Q4 - Confidence ratings**: "Sure but wrong" only works if students rate their confidence before they see the answer. Some parts of the app save no rating today. Should the drill always ask for one?

Recommended: ask on every drill question, as one optional tap that doesn't block the answer.

---

**Q5 - The session brief**: Before a session, a student picks up to 3 questions and adds a short note. It stays private until they tap Share, and then you see it in the Hub. Do you want this?

Recommended: yes.

---

**Q6 - Two questions from earlier:**
- (a) Keep today's full left menu instead of slimming it, since slimming changes every page.
- (b) With little data, show one result instead of the circles. With no practice, show an invitation to start. With no session or assignment, hide that card instead of showing an empty box.

Recommended: yes to both.

---

**Q7 - What goes in the first build**:

Recommended:
- **First build:** v3 layout, #6 honest visuals, #7 quality bar, #2 your note and Ask, #1 diagnosis to action, and #5 sure but wrong.
- **Second build:** the Today sequence, the session brief, and review with a fresh check. These need new saved data.
